Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tissue module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 820. The projective resolution has length at most 681 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the tissue space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 820 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 681. QED.
